富邦 /科技  
專家座談會議

等表示，亦無建議或推薦本文提及之任何證券，有關觀點亦未考量個別投資人之投資目標，富邦證券法人業務處不對投資損益
負擔任何責任。

座談重點摘要 
1. 原物料行情主導全年主軸，石油美元體系面臨重構：中東戰爭推升油價六成，金屬、農產
品、航運同步走揚，今年是「萬物起漲」年，賣原物料國家貨幣走強，買原物料國家貨幣
走弱。產油國因戰爭已無餘力購買美債，加上美元武器化制裁破壞信任，外資持有美債比
例已自 50%降至 33%。未來石油交易將形成美元、歐元、人民幣三元並立，黃金將持續受
惠於去美元化趨勢。 
2. 美國經濟靠股市撐消費，AI 驅動產業生產力提升但裁員潮蔓延：美國消費成長率 2%高於實
質可支配收入 1%，差額由股市財富效應補足。去年裁員 120 萬人規模等同金融海嘯，今年
Meta、JPMorgan 持續削減人力，但企業生產力卻創新高，主因疫情期間數位化加上頁岩油
壓低能源成本（電價僅歐洲一半）。AI 將使傳統產業出現「拉撒路效應」起死回生，但須
觀察六大金融風險，特別是美債安全性、回購市場槓桿與私募基金透明度。 
3. 台灣 AI 動能強勁但結構失衡，須借鏡中東主權基金模式分散風險：台灣今年 GDP 上修至
7%，外銷出口成長 15%，物價受凍漲政策壓制於 2%以下，央行無升息壓力。然而資通訊、
電子產品出口占比達 80%，資源過度集中於科技業，傳產與中南部產業相對弱勢。1980 年
2026 全球經濟情勢與產業景氣展望 Fubon Global Equity Division 
2026 年 05 月 28 日 
專家簡介 
台北大學企業管理博士，現任台灣經濟研究院景氣預測中心主任、人工智
慧科技基金會董事、三三青年會財經顧問。著作《孫主任的經濟筆記：景
氣預測權威的 12 堂總經課》 長期分析全球及台灣景氣，協助台灣重要產
業公會／法人組織訓練財經相關人才，並擔任金融、鋼鐵、石化及科技製
造等多家標竿企業及公協會經濟諮詢顧問。另多年從事調查研究，協助中
小企業找尋商機、開拓國際市場、轉型升級，並據此出版《中堅實力》系
列書籍共四本。

代日本半導體曾遭美國以半導體協定整治後一蹶不振，台灣須警覺 TSMC 海外設廠與訂單分
散風險。長期解方在於以上一代科技業獲利扶植下一代新創產業，而非短期補貼消耗。

會議內容 
1. 全球經濟分歧，北美獨強，新興市場承壓：根據 IMF 4 月預測，今年僅北美經濟成長率較
去年提升（1.9%→2.2%），其他區域包括歐洲、亞洲、新興市場皆呈現走弱態勢。美國經
濟動能來自去年通過之減稅法案、Fed 去年 9、11、12 月三次降息，以及新任聯準會主席
的鴿派傾向。川普所有政策動作將與期中選舉高度連動，金融市場若出現劇烈波動，川普
政策必然轉向刺激股市與經濟。過去十幾二十年新興市場「賭他都對」的邏輯已不適用，
外有貿易戰、內有經濟結構調整壓力，今年布局須留意各國強弱勢差異。 
2. 中東戰局推升萬物起漲，原物料行情主導全年主軸：戰爭爆發後石油價格自 60 美元漲至
90 美元，漲幅約六成，但只要不破百二、百四對經濟尚可承受。油價溫和上漲反而扮演經
濟助攻角色，刺激民眾積極消費。金屬端因中國去年起調整鋼鐵、銅、鋁等產能（中國佔
全球產量一半），中鋼等鋼鐵類股逐步回溫。農產品因生質燃料與化肥（天然氣為主要原
料）價格連動上揚。航運業則受惠於荷姆茲海峽、曼德海峽、蘇伊士運河等運輸路線受
阻。今年的拉貨潮分兩條主線：AI 帶動科技拉貨，以及傳產因原物料漲價的補庫存需求。 
3. 中東戰爭三大新血管：兩條已堵，第三條岌岌可危：伊朗與其盟友控制了三條全球能源與
貿易要道。第一條荷姆茲海峽每日運油量 1,300-1,500 萬桶，台灣六成石油與三分之一天
然氣仰賴此通道；第二條曼德海峽由胡塞組織控制，目前尚未封鎖；第三條蘇伊士運河為
亞歐貿易樞紐。波斯灣內三戶「死巷子人家」科威特、巴林、卡達受創最深，呈現雙位數
衰退；阿聯酋因阿布達比港口位於灣外受創較輕，但其依靠杜拜發展的航空、觀光、金

融、AI 資料中心（原規劃 18 座）已遭重創。沙烏地阿拉伯與阿曼因兩面臨海受影響相對
有限。 
4. 川普打不下去的關鍵：飛彈經濟學的消耗戰困境：伊朗彈道飛彈一顆 100 萬美元，無人機
僅 5 萬美元，且擁有地下工廠持續生產；美方薩德飛彈一顆 1,000 萬美元，愛國者一顆
500 萬美元，且通常需兩枚才能攔截一枚來襲飛彈。此種武器體系無法支撐長期消耗戰，
與韓戰、越戰、阿富汗戰爭的歷史模式如出一轍。美國從未輸過單一戰役，卻往往輸掉整
場戰役。只要對手能撐過兩個月，美國通常認輸。對岸亦看清此一弱點，未來對付美國的
策略將是長期消耗戰。 
5. 石油美元體系崩解，石油「三元」時代來臨：過去產油國將賣油美金回流購買美債，支撐
美元霸權與流動性循環。然而 2010 年外資持有美債比例由 50%降至 33%，今年 3 月日本
拋售逾 600 億美元、中國、沙烏地、台灣亦同步減持。當前產油國因戰爭已無餘力購債，
加上美國頻頻使用美元武器化制裁俄羅斯、伊朗，破壞了體系信任。未來石油交易將分散
為美元、歐元、人民幣三元並立。投資布局上應思考美債之外的替代資產，黃金將因不受
國際制裁干預而持續水漲船高，中國央行已大舉增持即為印證。 
6. 美國消費由股市撐起，AI 推升生產力：美國消費成長 2%，但實質可支配收入僅成長 1%
多，差額由股市財富效應彌補。費半、台股、韓股均在戰前已大漲，只要股市維持，消費
就維持。美國去年裁員 120 萬人規模相當於金融海嘯，今年 Meta、JPMorgan 等持續裁
員，但企業生產力卻在提升：資訊業生產率 2019-2024 提高 8%，主因疫情期間數位化轉
型，以及 2018 年後頁岩油讓美國電價僅歐洲一半、日本三分之一。AI 將使許多傳統產業
「拉撒路效應」起死回生，重點不在投資 AI 本身，而在業務能否與 AI 結合。 
7. AI 裁員潮中四種「人類保留」職業：依國際勞工組織研究，AI 難以取代四類工作：一是助
理工作；二是大金額、需信任感、需負法律責任的複雜銷售（保險、不動產）；三是研究

分析工作的最終呈現環節（資料整理可被取代，但現場演講與信任建立仍需人類）；四是
程式設計師。AI 對金融、教育、資訊、媒體四大產業衝擊最大。中小企業導入 AI 速度較
慢，因現有設備需折舊完畢才會換新，類似 19 世紀末蒸汽機到電力的過渡期。 
8. 今年六大金融風險清單：第一，安全的陷阱：美債不再安全，矽谷銀行即為前車之鑑。第
二，泡沫的反思：19 世紀鐵路泡沫留下鐵路網，2000 年網路泡沫留下光纖網路，AI 泡沫
後的電力與伺服器投資將成為下一代享受者的基礎。第三，回購市場槓桿過高：銀行不敢
放貸給民眾，轉而買美債質押借錢，槓桿極高，Fed 縮表或升息將引爆。第四，複雜商品
陷阱：類似 2008 年次貸的新型企業債打包商品。第五，新瓶裝舊酒：比特幣、穩定幣等
無央行、無資產支撐的貨幣。第六，私募基金不透明：財務不透明、流動性受限，台灣的
土建融轉道租賃公司亦屬類似結構性風險。 
9. 美債暴跌的三重壓力與美日英聯動：美債承壓來自三方面：荷姆茲海峽推升油價導致通膨
預期回升、財政部拍賣連續受挫（外資與美國金融業均不敢承接）、以及美日英三國公債
市場高度連動。日本若需護盤就須拋售美債，導致美債、英債、日債同步下跌。未來觀察
美債風險不能僅看美國本身，須留意日本央行升息或匯率干預動作的外溢效應。 
10. 中國房市恐步日本後塵，股市資金來源轉向：中國房市自 2022 年起跌三年多，少子化、
高齡化程度甚於日本（年減 3,000 多萬勞動力），房價難以如美國 5 年見底，恐走日本 20
年長期調整路徑。政府轉而刺激股市，本次行情與 2015 年國企改革不同，主軸為科技類
股，且資金來源並非散戶槓桿，而是將房地產資金驅趕至股市（中國民眾資產配置 70%在
房地產、30%在金融資產，與美國相反）。法人與 ETF 比重提升，市場機構化降低政策剎
車難度。中國、香港、韓國三地同步運用此一策略。 
11. 日本汽車產業崩解，國防產業接棒：Honda 去年虧損 43 億美元，為 1957 年以來首次全年
虧損；Nissan 2028 年前將關閉七座工廠、削減四成產能。日本全球車市佔有率自 31%降至

26%，中國市佔自 20%降至 10%。日本電動車轉型受限於供應鏈包袱（8%國民從事汽車
業），早年 Nissan Leaf 即遭股東反對而停滯。轉機在國防產業：高市政府已開放飛彈、裝
甲、巡防艦銷售，澳洲首張訂單為 11 艘軍艦。三菱重工、川崎重工等工業類股將成為日
股新主軸。 
12. 韓國股市槓桿高築，李在明押注科技強國：韓國融資餘額達 7,000 多億元，遠高於台灣
4,000 多億，未成年人開戶 72 萬戶（節稅考量），退伍軍人專案貸款 10%利率仍搶買。三
分之一國民資金投入股市。李在明完全執政後，直接撥 70 兆韓元紓困，並將首爾南邊龍
仁打造為 AI 超級聚落，要求 Samsung、SK Hynix 垂直整合半導體設備、材料供應鏈，擺脫
日本制約。未來十年韓國恐成為台灣科技業強勁對手，與日本上下游互補關係不同，韓國
是全面性競爭。 
13. 台灣經濟亮眼，央行升息壓力有限：台灣今年經濟成長率上修至 7%，外銷出口成長 15%
（1-4 月大爆發），1-4 月物價僅漲 1.35%，未達央行 2%警戒線。油價衝擊僅推升 0.3 個百
分點，預估全年仍低於 2%。關鍵在於凍漲政策：中油吸收虧損維持油價不漲，物價自然
壓得住。輸入型通膨無法以升息治理，升息只能治內需，故央行維持利率合理。年底選舉
考量下，油電雙漲為政治禁忌。 
14. AI 三波財富傳導，重點從硬體走向應用：借鏡蒸汽動力（1760-1910）與電力時代（1900-
1980）的歷史，重要發明的價值不在發明本身，而在其應用擴散。AI 目前僅推進兩年（保
守估計仍有 18 年榮景），市場過度聚焦上游硬體。未來投資邏輯應轉向：第一，電力效
率（每度電產出多少 token）；第二，算力商業模式（按量收費已成主流，重點是 token
產出而非投資金額）；第三，模型效率（Claude、Gemini、OpenAI 比較）；第四，應用擴
散。中國第 15 個五年規劃聚焦全行業 AI 應用（找 Minimax 老闆而非 DeepSeek），美國則
仍停留在模型層。AI 上游可能泡沫，但下游百花齊放才是長期主軸。

Q&A 
Q：去年新台幣於 5 月到 7 月出現失控升值，央行被指責升幅過大。以目前順差與資金流狀
況，如何看待新台幣後續走勢？ 
A：去年的失控源於關稅議題。當時台灣代表團赴美協商，外資判斷央行不敢阻擋新台幣升
值，因此啟動投機性買盤，過去新台幣升值 1 元需一個月，當時 1-2 天即達成。然而今年 AI 並
非政治議題，川普並未以美元匯率作為談判籌碼，他要的是 TSMC 赴美設廠等實質讓步，與過
去共和黨總統策略不同。央行已掌握此一態度。加上中東戰爭推升油價，美國作為產油國，美
元同步走強，新台幣維持在 31 元區間。中東戰爭結束、油價回落後，下半年新台幣才有升值
空間，但不會重演去年一天升值 1 元的劇本。年底選舉考量下，央行操作將更為謹慎，賴政府
票倉在中南部，中期選情不容匯率劇烈波動干擾。下半年若 Fed 降息、台灣利率不動，台美利
差縮小亦將支撐新台幣回升。

Q：台灣短期之間股市市值增加 100 兆元，但中南部許多非半導體產業並未受惠。整體經濟與
民生影響如何評估？ 
A：產業結構問題並非今年才出現。三五年前台灣最大外銷品為零組件，銷往中國組裝手機、
筆電，當時最關注 Apple 新機發表。近三年此一邏輯已淡出，台灣改為在地組裝伺服器後直接
出口美國（晶片管制使然） 。目前資通訊、電子、零組件出口占比達80%，傳產比重持續萎
縮。資源過度集中於科技業，年輕人才、資金、政府產業會議全部圍繞科技業，傳產與金融業
皆未受邀。下半年若 AI 動能不如預期，或美國成功扶植 Intel、Samsung 分散 TSMC 訂單，風險
即浮現。1980 年代日本半導體曾如日中天，後因美日半導體協定一蹶不振，僅能靠汽車、家電
維持。台灣解方在於以上一代賺的錢培育下一代新創公司，而非用於發放補貼（如普發 5,000
元） 。中東國家以石油美元成立主權基金發展教育、AI 資料中心，即為借鏡。

### Page 7

富邦 /科技  
專家座談會議

免責聲明：本文非研究報告，係由富邦證券法人業務處整理提供，所載資料僅供定客戶參考，不構成要約、招攬、宣傳、誘使
等表示，亦無建議或推薦本文提及之任何證券，有關觀點亦未考量個別投資人之投資目標，富邦證券法人業務處不對投資損益
負擔任何責任。

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 6 月 4 日

1 群益投顧 
台達電 (2308 TT) 於 COMPUTEX 2026 以「 Superior Efficiency, Shaping Sustainable 
AI 」 為 主 題 ， 提 出 多 項 創 新 解 決 方 案。 燃 料 電 池 與 固 態 變 壓 器 為 長 期 成 長 動 能 ，預期
2~3 年內將有較顯著營收貢獻 。台 達 電 為 AI 伺服器電源解決方案與水冷 Sidecar 主要
供 應 商，受 惠 於 AI、Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長，營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢，維 持Buy
投 資 評 等 ， 目 標 價 為 2,800 元 。

研 究 員 ： 王 美 珍   
iris.wang@capital.com.tw

燃 料 電 池 與 固 態 變 壓 器為 長 期 成 長 動 能：  
燃 料 電 池 具 備 高 效 率 、 可 堆 疊 特 性 。 公 司 已 規 劃 於 觀 音 廠 於2026 年 底 前 完
成 第 一 條 生 產 線 安 裝 ， 並 已 在 籌 備 觀 音 二 廠 擴 大 規 模 。 
在未來的 2~3 年 內 ， 包 含 燃 料 電 池 預 計 僅 佔 總 營 收約 1% ， 但 其 作 為 長 期 能
源 解 決 方 案 的 成 長 曲 線 非 常 明 確 。 
SST 目 前 已 少 量 出 貨 ， 客 戶 為 中 系 CSP 、 800V 高 壓 直 流 電 動 車 充 電 站 ， 亦
出 貨 給 美 系 客 戶 測 試 。SST 導 入 需 從 資 料 中 心 設 計 初 期 即 納 入 規 劃 ， 預 期 
2~3 年 後 會 產 生 顯 著 的 營 收 貢 獻 ， 仍 持 續 改 良 產 品 規 格 中 。 
雖然 SST 可 能 影 響 傳 統 AC/DC 電 源 業 務，但 SST 單 價 遠 高 於 電 源產品 ，應
是有利 。 台 達 電 在 AC/DC 電 源 業 務 市 佔 率極 高， 目前 亦 為 SST 進 度 最 快 的
廠商 ， 預 期 台 達 電 在 SST 仍 可 維 持 高市 佔 率 。  
台 達 電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 與 水 冷 Sidecar 主 要 供 應 商 ：  
為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商 。 受 惠 於 AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動 AC/DC
電 源 大 幅 成 長 外 ，CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC 電 源 模
組，亦 將 成 為 另 一 成 長 動 能。 NVIDIA 主 導 的 800 VDC 進 度 提 早 一 年，預 計
2026 年 生 產 並 出 貨 ；± 400VDC  2026 年 亦 會出貨。  
水冷散熱 以 Sidecar 為主 ， 2025 年、 1Q26 營收比重為 9% 。 台 達 電 Sidecar
產品 2025 年 市 佔 率 極 高， 雖 有 競 爭 者 加 入， 但 整 體 市 場 需 求 持 續 增 長 ， 故
2026 年 Sidecar 營 收 仍 將 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
台達電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案與 水冷 Sidecar 主 要 供 應 商， 受惠於 AI 、
Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長，營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢，維持 Buy 投 資 評 等，
目標價為 2,800 元 。

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因

價基礎由 26F移至28F， 反映本輪被動元件漲價 、 稼動率回升與費用槓桿效益可望延續至2027

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/06/03 收盤價： 820.0 ➢ 國巨 26Q1 營運延續成長，26Q2 稼動率高於季初指
引。26Q1 營收 381.7 億元，QoQ +6.1%、YoY +22.7%，
毛利率 38.1%，EPS 3.90 元。26Q2 標準品與高階品
稼動率均高於季初法說指引，顯示需求回補已由鉭電容
延伸至 MLCC、電阻與電感等主要產品線。 
➢ Rubin平台升級推升 MLCC用量， 日韓產能排擠使國巨
受惠。VR200單機 MLCC美元含量與顆數均較 GB300
明顯增加，高容值 MLCC 佔比同步提升。日韓廠產能
優先配置高階 AI產品後，一般 IT 與中低階元件供給轉
緊。國巨在標準品、中高階 MLCC 及一般 IT 應用布局
完整，受惠重點在稼動率提升與報價環境改善。 
➢ 鉭電容漲價趨勢延續，提供非 MLCC 獲利支撐。國巨
透過 KEMET取得鉭質電容領先地位，AI 伺服器需求使
鉭電容訂單能見度延伸。鉭原料成本上行亦提供報價調
整基礎。 
➢ 預估 2026/27/28 年 EPS 為 17.38/23.80/29.18 元、
2026/27/28年營收 1,667.58（YoY +25.45%）、2,004.02
（YoY +20.18%）、2,382.22 億元（YoY +18.87%） 。受
惠 AI 需求帶動高階 MLCC/鉭電容供給趨緊、價格調
漲，以及產能排擠效應延續，研究部上修2026 至 2028
年獲利預估，並將預估此循環將延續至 2028F。考慮同
業 PER 評價區間及國巨產業定位，目標價 1,020 元
（35x 28(F) PER） ， 潛在上漲空間24.4%， 評等維持 「逢
低買進」 。

營 運 分 析 
國巨為全球被動元件龍頭，併購驅動一站式解決方案平台 
國巨股份有限公司成立於 1977 年，是全球領先的被動元件製造商，產品涵
蓋晶片電阻、積層陶瓷電容（MLCC）、鉭質電容、電感、電解電容、高頻
天線與感測器等，廣泛應用於車用電子、工業控制、通訊設備與 AI 伺服器
領域。目前國巨為全球最大晶片電阻與鉭質電容供應商，並居全球第三大  
MLCC 與電感製造商，擁有完整的產品組合與全球製造據點。 
近年來，國巨積極 透過海外併購與策略性投資擴大營運版圖，包含併購  
KEMET、Pulse、君耀及奇力新等廠商，跨足類比 IC 與功率元件領域。2025 
年 Q4 進一步完成收購日本 Shibaura（全球熱敏電阻市佔第一），結合先
前併入的 Nexensos（PTC 溫度感測器），在感測器領域形成完整產品線。 
Q1產品營收結構為磁性元件 25.2%、鉭質電容 24.3%、MLCC 19.0%、電
阻 13.5%、感測器 13.0%、其他 5.0%；25 年終端應用以工業 28.9% 佔
比最高，其次為電腦及企業系統 25.2%、車用 20.1%、消費電子 12.2%、
電信 10.3%、航太軍工醫療 3.4%。產品組合中高階品佔比約  75%、標準
品約 25%。區域別方面，美國 30.4%、亞洲 （不含中國）26.0%、中國 23.0%、
歐洲 20.5%；通路結構為經銷商 39.9%、OEM 37.8%、EMS 22.3%。 
圖一、國巨(2327) 2026Q1產品別營收比重。

資料來源：國巨，兆豐國際彙整

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操

- 3 - 
個股報告 
Rubin平台升級推升 MLCC用量，國巨受惠供給排擠效應 
AI伺服器平台由 GB300進入 Rubin VR200後，單機架 MLCC用量提升 80%
與美元含量明顯提升 1.8 倍。量增主要來自 compute board 與 switch board，
新一代模組可望帶來的額外用量。更重要的是規格組合改變，高容值 MLCC
佔比提升後，將占用日韓廠較多高階產能資源。 
國巨並非高容值 AI MLCC的主要領導者 ， 這點反而使本輪受惠邏輯與日韓廠
不同。日韓廠直接承接高階AI MLCC需求，國巨則受惠其產能被排擠後，提
供一站式一般 IT、標準品與中高階 MLCC/被動元件解決方案。在消費性電子
衰退預期下，此優勢在同業中隨稼動率提升與交期拉長，國巨的議價環境轉
佳。 
與 2018 年不同，目前市場未見恐慌性囤貨的跡象。前次報告已提到，經銷
商 months on hand 已較一年前下降，且客戶以穩定供應為前提接受調價，
而非競相囤貨。這使本輪價格調整較接近供需正常化後的毛利修復，而不是
單純庫存循環。

鉭電容漲價趨勢延續，非 MLCC產品提高獲利穩定性 
鉭質電容仍為國巨本輪獲利上修的重要來源。AI伺服器功率密度提升，板面
空間受限，使鉭質電容在電源管理與高可靠度應用中的重要性提高。國巨透
過 KEMET 取得鉭電容領先地位，具備較高定價能力，且鉭電容訂單能見度
已延伸至下半年。 
研究部將鉭電容視為長期受需求成長、成本上升與供給結構影響的產品線。
原料成本上升有助於支撐價格調整，AI相關應用需求增加則帶動終端需求。
若後續價格調整順利反映成本變化，鉭電容獲利能力仍有進一步改善空間。 
除鉭電容外，電阻與電感亦為後續獲利支撐。晶片電阻用量與MLCC具高度
連動，國巨具備高市占與成本優勢；電感則受惠 AI伺服器 power module、
車用與電信應用需求。非MLCC產品線同步回升，可降低市場對單一MLCC
循環的疑慮。

獲利預估上修反映費用槓桿與營運效率改善 
前次報告已反映 26Q1 財報優於研究部原先模型、鉭電容漲價分批進入損益
表，以及通路庫存回到健康水位，但對費用槓桿估計偏保守。當時模型較接
近營收、毛利與費用同步上調的假設，因此低估營收上修後對營業利益率的
貢獻。 
本輪營收增量主要來自價格調整、稼動率提升與產品組合改善，並不需要等
比例增加銷售、管理與研發費用。換言之，若同樣增加 100 元營收，本輪有
較高比例可留在營業利益。研究部預估營業費用率由 26F 的 12.1%下降至
28F 的 9.4%，營業利益率由 27.1%提升至 32.4%。這是造成先前 EPS與目
標價低估的主因。

### Page 4

- 4 - 
個股報告 
圖二、國巨(2327)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐國際預估

獲利預估上修，費用槓桿支撐評價調升 
研究部預估國巨 2026 年營收 1,667.6 億元，YoY +25.4%，毛利率 39.2%，
稅後淨利 360.2 億元，EPS 17.4元；2027 年營收 2,004.0 億元，YoY 
+20.2%，毛利率 41.2%，稅後淨利 493.5 億元，EPS 23.8元；2028年營收
2,382.2 億元，YoY +18.9%，毛利率41.8%，稅後淨利605.0 億元，EPS 29.2
元。 
本次上修是重新評估 2026 至 2028 年價格調整、產能排擠與費用槓桿對損益
表的影響。毛利率提升來自價格調整、稼動率回升及高階品占比提升；營業
利益率提升則來自費用率下降。估值方面，評價基礎由 26F 移至 28F，反映
本輪被動元件循環並非單季或單年度事件，上調目標價 1,020元（35x 28(F) 
PER），潛在上漲空間 24.4%，投資評等維持「逢低買進」。

林茂揚 
886-2-27815995#37018 
maoyoung.lin@fubon.com 
敬鵬 (2355 TT)

毛利率表現遇壓

維持中立評等，目標價 55 元。 
1. 車用回温及工業成長，維持 2026 年營收成長看法，估計 14%。 
2. 自結 4 月 EPS 0.13 元，保守看待 2Q26 毛利率表現。 
3. 通訊及航天營收比重下滑；估計泰國新廠最快 1H27 產出貢獻。

車用回温及工業成長，維持 2026 年營收成長看法，估計 14% 
歷經 4 年營收下滑後，公司維持年初對今年營收成長正向看法不變，其中成長動能
有二，一為油車需求好轉，公司表示原對車用產品需求成長 保守看待，唯今年歐、
美有些車廠又轉向油車發展動作，因此訂單水準及需求相對先前呈現好轉。二則是
工業相關新產品成長。整體來看，公司對 2026 年營收展望正向，至於成長幅度取
決於汽車市場回溫速度與工業電子新產品訂單進來的速度， 富邦估計年成長14%。

自結 4 月 EPS 0.13 元，保守看待 2Q26 毛利率表現 
1Q26 合併營收 40.56 億元 ，QOQ+1.6%，YOY+5.6%， 符合預期 ， 毛利率12.1%，
較前一季 14.0%及同期 12.6%呈現下滑，主要是受原物料上漲干擾，而費用控制得
宜，加上匯兌利益挹注，稅後淨利 2.47 億元，EPS 0.62 元。另 1Q26 用產品中，
車用需求回溫 ，1Q26 汽車佔由 75%提升至 77%， 另需求看俏的工業應用佔比維持
14%，通訊/航天比重由 6%進一步下滑至 5%，消費也由 5%下滑至 4%。此外，公
司自結 4 月單月 EPS 0.13 元，低於市場預期，主要是原料成本持續墊高，由於目前
漲價是與客戶成本分攤，幅度尚無法 100%完全轉嫁成本上漲，保守看待 2Q26 毛
利率表現，而未來不排除二度漲價。

通訊及航天營收比重下滑；估計泰國新廠最快 1H27 產出貢獻 
公司通訊及航天比重由 2024 年 9%，下滑至 2025 的 6%，1Q26 進一步下滑至
5%，公司表示，通訊及航天客戶目前出貨客戶非 SpaceX，且提供產品以較利基品
項為主，營收貢獻不大；至於泰國廠方面，1Q26 虧損 300 萬，主要因素有舊廠翻
新對產能、人力有影響，加上原物料成本上漲且多半進口，預期下半年營運及獲利
將改善，包括來自漲價效益。另泰國新廠方面，預計 2026 年底前完工，該廠預計
投資 30 億元，完成後最大產值約 30~40 億元，預計 1H27 產出貢獻，而該廠非單
一客戶專廠，產品應用也非單一。

富邦不同之處 
富邦為少數持續追蹤且出具更新報告券商之一。

評價與風險 
受惠車用市場需求回温及工業需求成長，營收展望正向，配合 LEO 題材，公司今年
來股價大漲，由 30 元一度上漲至最高 71 元，目前 P/E 29.6 倍及 P/B 1.4 倍，評價
來到歷史區間上緣，考量獲利受原物料干擾，加上LEO 佔比相對有限，短期評價上
調空間相對有限，因此暫維持中立評等，目標價則反應大盤上漲及同業水準上調至
55 元，約相當 2026 年的 1.24 倍 P/B。

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months 
Underweight  Sector expected to underperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months.

2603 長榮 ( EMC )

報告日 期： 2026 / 05 / 29 研 究 員 ： 林 奕 頎(Yichi Lin)  
壹、投 資 建 議 
一.美 伊 各 懷 鬼 胎 ， 拉 扯 協 議 ，以 拖 待 變 ，油價 及 航 商 獲 得 喘 息 
2 月 28 日 美 伊 戰 爭 爆 發 ， 海 運 燃 油 （ 新 加 坡 ） 從2 月 27 日每
噸 697 美 元 ， 狂 飆 至4 月 2 日每噸  1,789 美元， 上漲 157% 。 
後 續 ， 美 伊 進 行 停 火 協 商 ， 原 油 市 場 恐 慌 降 溫 ， 油 價 回 跌 。 
5 月 26 日 最 新 報 價， 海 運 燃 油 每 噸 1,048 美 元， 較 美 伊 戰 爭 爆
發 前 的 2 月 27 日 697 美 元 ，上漲 50% 。 
同 期 間，航 商 因 應市場 恐慌（ 擔 心 經 濟 衰 退 ），進 行 大 幅 減 班 ，
更 為 轉 嫁海 運 燃 油成 本 ， 大 幅 調 漲 運 價 ，SCFI 運 價 指 數 ， 從2
月 27 日 1,333 .11 一 路 走 升 至5 月 28 日 2,218.15 ，上漲 66%。 
油價暴漲，讓貨櫃航商驚慌，緊緊抱團，落實減班，大幅推升
運價， 超 額 轉 嫁 海 運 燃 油 成 本， 反 成 為 美 伊 戰 爭 受 惠 者。 
二.估 2Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 5. 97 元， YoY + 18%，QoQ + 56%  
1Q / 202 6 單季 SCFI 運 價 指 數 平 均 1,491 。 
2Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運 價 指 數 （ 至5 月 28 日 ） 平 均 1,961 。 
1Q / 202 6 單 季 海 運 燃 油 平 均 每 噸 840 美元 。 
2Q / 2026 單 季 海 運 燃 油（ 至5 月 26 日 ）平 均 每噸 1,238 美元 。 
附 註 ：5 月 28 日 SCFI 指數  2,218； 26 日 海 運 燃 油 1,048 美元。 
宏 遠 預 期，受 惠 季 節 性 轉 強 及 超 額 轉 嫁 燃 油，估計 長榮 2Q / 
2026 單 季 營 收QoQ + 152 億 元； 燃 油 支 出QoQ + 60 億 元； 推
升營業利益 QoQ + 74 億元 ； 單 季 稅 後EPS + 5.97 元 ， 優 於1Q 
/ 2026 的 + 3.84 元 ， 亦 優 於2Q / 2025 同 期 的 + 5.06 元。  
三.短 期 間 不 易 出 現 大 規 模 軍 事 衝 突 ，市 場 信 心 恢 復 ， 壓 抑 油 價 
伊 朗 面 對 經濟大幅衰退  及 石油停產 （ 毀 壞 油 田 ）的 壓 力 。 
美 國 面 對 戰爭權力決議  60 天限制  及 11 月 3 日 期 中 大 選。 
《 戰 爭 權 力 決 議 》 規 定 ， 若 總 統 未 獲 國 會 授 權 派 兵 作 戰 ，
必 須 在 60 天 內 向 國 會 請 求 授 權。 川普 3 月 2 日 向 國 會 報
告 ， 起 算60 天 ； 宣 稱4 月 7 日 已 停 戰 ， 無 須 國 會 授 權 。 
美伊雙方各有壓力，都需喘息空間，因此，雙方以拖待變的心
態濃厚，短期間，不易爆發大規模軍事衝擊，有利於恢復市場
信 心 ， 壓 抑 短 期 間 油 價 漲 勢 （長 期 則 必 須 觀 察 庫 存 水 準）。 
四.買進 ：季 節 性 轉 強 ； 美伊以拖待變 ； 2Q 及 3Q 獲 利 逐 季 走 高 
貨 櫃 航商， 面對 恐 慌 ， 緊 緊 抱 團 ， 大 幅 拉 高 運 價 ， 超 額 轉 嫁 燃
油 成 本 ； 同 時 ， 季 節 性 轉 強 ，即將進入 3Q 旺季；  此 外 ， 美
伊 雙 方 以 拖 待 變，有 利 恢 復 市 場 信 心，油 價 恐 慌短期 退 燒； 宏
遠 預 期 貨櫃航商 2Q 及 3Q 獲 利 逐 季 走 高； 建 議 買 進 長 榮。

船 隊 運 力 的5.9%）。

船隊 運 力 的 44.8 % 。

1. 美 伊 戰 爭 爆 發 ，航 商 抱 團 拉 抬 運 價 ， 超 額 轉 嫁海 運 燃 油成本  
長 榮 海 運1Q / 2026 ， 海 運 燃 油佔 合 併 營 業 成 本19% 。 
依照 1Q / 2026 的 成 本 結 構，假 設 其 他 條 件 不 變 ， 則 平均油價  每
上漲  10%， 平 均 運 價 僅 需 上 漲 2%， 即 可超額 轉 嫁 燃 油 上 漲 成 本。

1Q / 2026 單 季 海 運 燃 油 平 均 每 噸 840 美 元 。 
2Q / 2026 單 季 海 運 燃 油 （至 5 月 26 日 ） 平 均 每 噸 1,238 美元。  
2Q / 2026 ，單季海運燃油 QoQ + 47% 。

2Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運 價 指 數 （至 5 月 28 日 ）平均  1,961 。 
2Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運 價 指 數 QoQ + 32% 。

在 10% 油 價 漲 幅，僅 需不到  2% 運 價 漲 幅 即 可 轉 嫁 的 情 況 下 ：

商 已 超 額 轉 嫁 燃 油 上 漲 成 本，貨 櫃 航 商2Q 獲 利 將 明 顯 優 於1Q 。

此 外 ， 依 據 最 新 報 價 ， 貨 櫃 航 商 超 額 轉 嫁 的 情 況 更 為 嚴 重：

最 新 資 料，顯 示，運 價 持 續 上 漲，海 運 燃 油 持 續 下 跌，貨 櫃 航 商
超 額 轉 嫁 的 幅 度 不 斷 擴 大； 因 此 ， 可 以 合 理 預 期 貨 櫃 航 商2Q / 
2026 獲 利 將 明 顯 優 於1Q / 2026 。

## Document 054: 260528_gs_WNC

### Page 1

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
WNC (6285.TW): 800G switch in expansion; Buy with TP up to NT$364
28 May 2026 | 2:26PM HKT
WNC is benefiting from networking technology migrations. As a major ODM supplier
of network communications solutions with long-term experience, WNC enables rapid
shipment of new designs for its customers. We remain positive on WNC, riding on the
networking switch product expansion, including the Enterprise 800G switch
(contributing revenues in 2H26) and OCS switch (long-term opportunities), the

accelerating adoption of LEO satellite networks (Read more), and the WiFi-7
adoption cycle that is driving up replacement demand. We raise our 12-month target
price by 14.5% to NT$364.0 and maintain our Buy rating.
800G switch in expansion. As a leading ODM for networking equipment, WNC will
start mass shipment of 800G switch in 2H26. The 800G switch will be an ODM
product for branded customers, targeting enterprise clients as the major end users.

This supports our positive view on rising networking demand along with AI
computing, where 800G high-speed products have already expanded applications
from CSPs to enterprises. With ongoing networking technology migrations, we
expect 800G switch demand to continue into 2027 and may expand to 1.6T in the
future. Apart from the 800G switch, OCS switches are also a long term opportunity
(link
), as they are also innovative networking products that will greatly increase data

transmission efficienc y and in our view have the potential to become a mainstream
form factor in the future. We are positive that the continuous development of
advanced networking solutions will support WNC’s growth along with the ramping
trend of networking advancements in AI data centers.

### Page 2

Earnings revision: we raise WNC revenues by 1%/ 2%/ 2% for 2026-28E, based on a
more positive outlook on the high speed switch business. While we raise revenues by
1%/ 2%/ 2%, we make no changes to our GM and OPM estimates as we already factored
in the higher margin expectations of WNC as new products ramp up, with 2026-28E GM
at 13.5%+ (vs. 2025A GM of 12.5%).
Valuation: With the earning estimate changes and a refreshed target P/E ratio, we raise

our 12M TP by 14.5% to NT$364.0 (based on 28.0x 2027E P/E, vs. 25.0x previously).
We continue to base our TP on 2027E P/E, and the target multiple is still derived from
the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers (Exhibit 5), which is higher as we
mark to market. Our target P/E of 28.0x is higher than company’s avg. +1 std P/E (16.3x),
which we believe is not stretched considering the sustainable growth of WNC driven by
long-term satellite and high speed networking demand. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology: WNC
Valuation: We use a near-term P/E to derive our 12m target price for WNC, consistent
with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our TP of NT$364.0 on a target P/E
multiple of 28.0x on a forward year EPS (2027E). Our target P/E is derived from the
correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers. We are Buy rated on WNC. Key
downside risks: fier
cer-than-expected competition in satellite communication,
slower-than-expected Wi-Fi 7 or 5G FWA ramp-up in US and Europe markets.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

Fositek Corp  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026 Feedback
• 2Q26 revenue outlook stays at flat to up 5% QoQ as smartphone hinge
projects entered model transition. Gross margin should thus trend slightly
higher for favorable mix shifting server application.
• 3Q26 revenue outlook should reflect new project rollout benefits for at
least 10% QoQ increase and the growth magnitude depends on new GPU
platform volume ramp progress.
• Fositek should benefit from quick disconnect (QD) supply share gain for new

VR GPU server racks with offerings extended to the compute tray.
• AI ASIC exposure is expanding with new projects in the pipeline from 2H26.
This includes the QD connecting to the cold plate modules for GPU and CPU
as well as optical transceivers (by end of 2026).
• Fositek has implemented automated production lines in China and Vietnam
for total four lines in 1H26.  This should help drive its gross margin higher in
2H26 onward.
• Server rail kit offerings are mainly for general purpose servers now and

Fositek expects further inroads to AI GPU server racks and standalone power
racks in 2H26.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Update
King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026
Takeaways
• Rail kit demand: Management sees it coming from a variety of applications,
including server racks, switch racks, power racks, CPI, rack, storage racks, etc.
Management is confident to maintain the dominant share in the rail kit
market for both GPU and ASIC servers in the coming year, as it will stay as
the design partner for almost all new projects extending beyond 2029.

• GM trend
: It is likely to see modest expansions in 2026. Longer term, its
comprehensive patent portfolio and solid execution should help hold its GM
at an elevated level, since peers are less likely to catch up under the much
shorter design cycle.
•  Capacity expansion pipeline
: There is still plenty of capacity to support
growth in the near term, off the record high revenue of NT$2596mn (+35%
MoM/+79% YoY) in April, and more capacity expansions are in the pipeline.

US capacity will start mass production from September 2026 and customers
are willing to pay for the incremental costs, so there will be no impact to
King Slide’s margin.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 34x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 30% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 1.2.
This is in line with the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E

vs. an operating profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

M
Update
ASMedia Technology Inc  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026 Feedback
Financials: The full-year revenue split between AMD and non-AMD is around
50%/50%. Full-year GM target is 50-55%, skewed towards the low end. Opex ratio
is expected to be 20-25% for the full year.
Chipset business: AM4/5 revenue mix this year could  be 50%/50% (vs. last year
25%/75%), due to weaker demand for AM5. The PC market is expected to decline by
15-20% this year, from a combination of weak demand from memory price hikes and

CPU shortage. AM4 uses DDR4, which is easier to procure than DDR5 used by AM5.
A new platform is expected in 2H27 with ~20% ASP hikes.
PCIe
: Gen5 switch is expected to tape out in 2H26, with Gen6/7 technology
currently under development. PCIe revenue contribution is around 10% this year, vs.
mid-single digit last year, and is likely to double again next year.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
ASMedia Technology Inc (5269.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity of 9.8% (beta 1.3, risk-free
rate 2% and risk premium 6%), medium-term growth rate of 8.0%, payout ratio of 75% and
terminal growth rate of 5.2%.
Risks to Upside
n AMD gains market share thanks to better-than-expected performance.
n In-house designed products grow faster than expected.

n US-China tensions improve and US tariffs alleviate.
Risks to Downside
n AMD cannot get substrate supply, causing earnings downside for ASMedia.
n Intel adopts TSMC's technologies more aggressively to compete with AMD.
n Spec migration on USB 4 is slower.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 086: 260601_gs_ZDT

Source PDF: all report pdf/260601_gs_ZDT.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-01

Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C., Taipei
Branch
Zhen Ding Technology Holding (4958.TW): Taiwan Computex &
Corporate Day: Multi-year expansion to support strong AI
1 June 2026 | 10:35PM CST
We hosted meetings with Zhen Ding at our Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day
(June 1st). Key takeaways include:
(1) Upward revision on IC substrate/AI server/optical business: Management has
revised up its guidance on IC substrate/AI server/optical business to reach 40-45% of

total revenue by 2030 (previous guidance was 20-25%), and expects these
businesses to reach 25% in 2026 (was ~12% in 2025).
(2) IC substrate business with strong momentum: Management has revised up its
IC substrate business YoY growth from 60% to 80% in 2026 with strong visibility
extended into 2027. ABF substrate business has turned profitable in 1Q26, while ABF
plant 2 in ShenZhen will begin contributing from 2H26 and the company will
continue to expand non-China ABF substrate capacity.

(3) Ongoing progress with mSAP solutions for 1.6T optical: The company is
currently sampling mSAP solutions for 1.6T optical and expects to reach MP starting
from 3Q26. Optical revenue is expected to account for 4% of total revenue in 2026
(vs. 0.4% in 2025). Management mentioned that yield is currently at 80%+, and is
improving toward 90%, and believes the leading yield vs. peers is the key reason that
the company can capture leading market share in optical mSAP solutions amid

supply shortage.
(4) Massive multi-year CAPEX cycles supported by customer demand:
Management reiterated its 2026 CAPEX guidance of NT$80bn, driven by customer
orders and long-term agreements. This year’s CAPEX is primarily for expanding HDI
and higher layer count PCBs (>65% of CAPEX), with the remainder allocated between
IC substrates (15-20%) and FPC (~15%). The company also mentioned CAPEX
spending is expected to remain high at similar levels throughout 2028-30.

(5) Consumer business remains stable while mSAP shifts toward AI: The
consumer business remains core, but as IC substrate/AI server/optical scale up,
overall seasonality in GM is expected to flatten in 2026 (was seeing 80ppts quarterly
GM variation in 2025). mSAP was previously mainly used in smartphones, but is
expected to shift toward a 50:50 split between smartphone and optical/AI server in
next 3 years driven by the strong demand.
(6) IC substrate subsidiary going public: Zhen Ding’s IPO plan for its IC substrate

subsidiary, Leading Tech, has passed board approval, with documentation to be
delivered in 2H26, followed by a planned IPO in Hong Kong in 2027.
Goldman Sachs does and seeks to do business with companies covered in its research reports. As a result, investors
should be aware that the firm ma y have a conflict of interest that could affect the objectivity of this report.
Investors should consider this report as only a single factor in making their investment decision. For Reg AC

www.gs.com/research/hedge.html
. Analysts employed by non-US affiliates are not registered/qualified as research
analysts with FINRA in the U.S.

### Page 2

Outlook
We are positive on ZDT’s IC substrate/AI server/optical business outlook as management
guided for these businesses to contribute 25% of total revenue in 2026. We expect AI
server demand to a
ccelerate in 2H26, supported by the company’s successful PCB
verifica tion for Vera Rubin’s compute tray, and we expect the contribution to become
more meaningful in 2H26-1H27, with better profitability (a
verage OPM of 20-30% for AI
computing tray vs. company overall OPM of 10-15%), assuming the company continues

to deliver good yield. Furthermore, we expect to see increasing contribution from both
ASIC-related projects (from AWS and Google) and optical solutions (with PCB for 1.6T
optical module to see small volume starting in 2Q26) in 2026. We expect to see more
ABF substrate contribution upon the completion of ShenZhen Plant 2 and the company
continues to expand non-China capacity.
The company reiterated its 2026 CAPEX guidance of NT$80bn+ (implying 150%+ YoY),

as the company expects to complete 10 factories this year as part of its long-term
strategy to build 15 factories. The company guided that 65% of 2026 CAPEX will be
allocated for the server and optical business, 15% to the IC substrate business, and 10%
to the PFC business. The proactive investment plan is in line with our positive view that
AI PCB will see 140% 2025-27E CAGR (see here
), and we believe ZDT is well-positioned
to be one of the winners in the AI era considering its strong execution track record and

proactive capacity expansion plan relative to peers.
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$388 is based on a SOTP valuation methodology,
including (1) 2.6x blended 4Q26-3Q27E P/B for ABF & BT substrate, (2) 20x
4Q26-3Q27E P/E for high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in line with industry
average), and (3) 8x 4Q26-3Q27E P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB (in-line
with industry downcycle average valuation).
Key downside risks include: (1) weaker-than-expected iPhone demand or slower

content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate
ramp up progress, and (3) fier
cer competition in China ABF market.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

by Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research and referred to in this research.

Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Profitability turnaround in April
Turning profitable in April: Powerchip reported Apr 2026 net profit of NT$552mn,
+243.46% Y/Y; April 2026 EPS of NT$0.13, +244.44% Y/Y. Apr 2026 net margin was
10.94%. This is not comparable to 1Q26 net margin, which was skewed by NT
$20.1bn gains from the Fab P5 disposal and NT$5.5bn related impairments booked in
1Q. Excluding these gains and impairments, 1Q26 net margin would be -2.7%.

Therefore,  April's profit reflects the company actually turning profitable excluding
one-off items. The 10.94% margin is also stronger than our 2Q26e net margin
estimate of 7.1%. April EPS of NT$0.13 represents 66% of our 2Q26e EPS estimate
of NT$0.29.
Reiterate OW
:  we believe Powerchip is a beneficiary of mature node up-cycle and

benefit from the mature mode up-cycle, and the volume ramp-up of the company's

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Powerchip Semiconductor Manufacturing Co (6770.TW)
Base case, 2.1x 2026e P/B. This method is in line with our approach for Greater China memory
IDM players, in view of the industry's high volatility. Our 2.1x target P/B is higher than the
historical average of 1.5x since 2021, reflecting our optimistic view on improved pricing and
utilization.
Risks to Upside
n Pricing power is sustained in 2026.

n Listing on main board attracts investment fund flows.
n Aluminum process enjoys higher margins.
Risks to Downside
n Competition from China intensifies.
n Price erosion is faster than expected.
n Demand from consumer electronics clients weakens further.
n Inventory in the supply chain takes longer to digest.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 095: 260601_ms_yageo

Source PDF: all report pdf/260601_ms_yageo.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-01
Pages: 25
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
 Idea
Why has the stock rallied so much over the past month? There is fear that leading 
Japanese and Korean MLCC suppliers are shifting more capacity to cater to the high 
end,  resulting in undersupply for the midrange/low end. The same narrative that 
drove the 2018 cycle is now back in play. 
So what is happening?

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
§ = Consensus data is provided by Refinitiv Estimates
e = Morgan Stanley Research estimates

### Page 3

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
Investment Thesis
What does the supply/demand balance look like? Mapping out supply and demand has
always been very difficult for the MLCC market. Companies typically do not disclose their
capacity data; even for companies that do, it is often disclosed in amount of units per
month, or per year. However, there are different case sizes and layer counts for MLCC,
which all need to be taken into account when trying to calculate  supply. Similarly, this

becomes a challenge when modeling demand as well. Thus, it is hard to build a bottom-up
supply/demand model. That said...
Here is what we are hearing from the supply chain
• Utilization rates are increasing... Yageo attended our Asia AI Summit last week,
and mentioned that 2Q utilization  for all standard and premium products is
running  higher than its guidance during the 1Q earnings call; currently standard
products are running at 75%+ and premium at 85%+. According to Yahoo news

today, Chairman Pierre Chen even mentioned at Yageo's recent AGM that
utilization rates will reach 80%+ for standard products and 90%+ for premium,
though he did not mention a specific time for when this will happen.
• ...so suppliers are expecting pricing increase for the midrange/low end: Lead-
times are also extending, and this gives suppliers better bargaining power with end
customers to raise prices for the midrange/low end, where prices and profitability

are not as good. As a result, we expect to see price increases for midrange/low-end
MLCCs, though not yet to the extent of 2018, when distributor pricing increased
~10x. For the high end, it appears that most suppliers are still waiting to see if
Murata raises prices and will only follow Murata. However, according to Shoji Sato,
Murata doesn't plan to raise prices for existing MLCC products.
• Distributors and ODMs are both building inventory: Our checks indicate that

both distributors and ODMs are asking for more MLCC and buying as much as they
can to build inventory. The distributors are scared of supply shortage, while the
ODMs are trying to make sure that MLCC does not become the next bottleneck in
the supply chain.
AI demand is growing
We estimate MLCC dollar content to rise ~182% for VR200 NVL72 vs. GB300 NVL72:
Based on our latest estimates, we estimate MLCC content for VR200 to be ~US$4.3K.
This would present a quite meaningful increase vs. GB300 at only ~US$1.5K, and could

explain why high-end AI server MLCC demand is currently so strong and causing all the
ODMs to aggressively trying to secure and build as much inventory as possible, ahead of
the Rubin rack ramp from 2H26 onwards. Our checks indicate that the MLCC content per
computing board and switch board is increasing quite meaningfully, with the computing
board MLCC content showing a larger content increase. In addition, the newly introduced
BlueField and ConnectX modules will also contribute to more MLCC content per rack.

Total MLCC unit demand is rising ~80% for a VR200 NVL72 rack vs a GB300 NVL72
rack: Based on our estimate, we think there is ~570K units of MLCCs within an entire

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
Rubin NVL72 rack, which is meaningfully higher than a GB300 NVL72 rack at ~320k units
of MLCCs ( Exhibit 2 ). This increase comes from increasing content per board for the
computing board and switch board, but also increased PCB content, such as the CX9
Orchid Module. This increase in unit count is one of the main drivers behind the 182%
increase in dollar content.
This  means that a higher proportion of high-cap MLCCs is driving the other ~100%

growth: We also see increasing demand for high-cap MLCCs (47uF+), where we see these
high-cap MLCCs rising to 30%+ of the mix in entire Rubin system. This would mean a
meaningful increase in demand vs. GB300 (<20%) –   Exhibit 3 . Under our assumption,
47uF+ MLCC demand would rise quite dramatically, from a unit perspective. Our estimate
would imply that 47uF+ MLCC demand would rise 210% for a VR200 rack vs. GB300,
while 47uF and below MLCC content would rise at a lower magnitude of ~50%.

Stronger demand may cause a crowding out of capacity for suppliers that are geared
more towards the high end: This meaningful increase in MLCC volume per rack,
especially that of high-cap MLCCs. is exactly what distributors and ODMs  fear: that this
would cause a crowding out of capacity for the midrange/low-end. With that said, Yageo is
relatively less levered to the high-cap/high-end MLCC market for AI servers, vs. its
Japanese or Korean peers. However, Yageo still has good exposure in AI servers for MLCCs

that are 22uF or below. In addition, Yageo also plays a crucial role in supplying tantalum
capacitors into AI servers – it is currently the largest tantalum capacitor supplier globally.
Tantalum revenue was ~22% in CY25 but we see that rising to ~25%+ in CY26-28. And in
terms of profits, tantalum capacitors also carry very high margins, by our estimate (45%+
at least), and so we expect profit to rise from <30% of the mix in CY25 to 30% or higher
in CY26-28.

For more analysis of our Rubin Rack BOM analysis, please see here: Greater China
Technology Hardware: Analysis of Rubin rack BOM, component content, and ODM value-
added (20 May 2026)

Source: Morgan Stanley Research estimates. Note: High-cap refers to 47uF+ MLCCs.
Tantalum capacitor pricing may show further price increases
in 2H
Exhibit 4 shows that the prices of tantalum pentoxide have been increasing since early

2025, and have risen even more sharply since early 2026. In response, Yageo has been
taking corresponding pricing actions to reflect the inflationary raw material costs as well –
it has announced pricing adjustments for its tantalum products at least three times since
April 2025, based on our supply chain checks. With tantalum pentoxide pricing having
risen 100%+ since late 2025, we believe Yageo is highly likely to implement another round
of price adjustments for its tantalum products in the near term, which should further

improve its profitability.

Raising estimates for Yageo
2Q tracking ahead of guidance: As mentioned above, utilization rate is tracking above its
previous guidance given during its 1Q earnings call. Hence, we raise our 2Q revenue
forecast 5%, to NT$41.9bn (+10% q/q) - 2% above the Street, with GM of 39.0% (+90bps
q/q) and OpM of 26.8% (+160bps q/q) for EPS of NT$4.21 –  1% above the Street.
We update our model to reflect the potential for, continued price hikes, primarily for

tantalum capacitors and MLCCs, driven by AI demand. Our 2026-28 EPS estimates
increase 7%, 23%, and 53%, respectively, driven by higher assumptions for both revenues
and margins.

### Page 10

M
 Idea
10
Key catalyst
Monthly revenue shows signs of acceleration: This is always something the market 
tends to focus on when trading MLCC stocks, and we are starting to see signs of 
acceleration at Yageo. With demand continuing to see improvement and if pricing 
adjustments happens in 2H, we expect to see further acceleration of monthly revenue, and 
this would be positive for the stock. We have observed this consistent relationship over 
the past decade, and we don’t think this time will be any different. 
Exhibit 7: Yageo's monthly sales y/y are starting to show signs of acceleration
Source: TEJ, Morgan Stanley Research.

Where there could be risks 
The obvious risk is a sudden bursting of demand for AI. However, the likelihood of this 
happening is relatively low, considering what we know today around increasing GPU rental 
prices, real use cases for AI, etc. Nonetheless, this would be the most obvious risk, but it 
also would affect almost all the companies in the supply chain, and not just MLCC players. 
That said, other areas we are monitoring are inventory risk and consumer electronics 
demand risk, particularly in 2H26-1H27. At our Asia AI Summit, Yageo mentioned that 
distributor inventory remains healthy at <5 months (vs. slightly above 5 months in 1Q). 
However. since inventory level is based on the current end demand, a meaningfully lower 
end demand environment would imply that the same amount of MLCC inventory for 
distributors would take much longer to clear out. In that scenario, pricing pressure would 
likely follow shortly after.

12
Price target discussion and valuation methodology
We raise our 12-month price target to NT$1,010 (from NT$360): This reflects our
higher earnings estimates for CY26-28 and our raised medium-term growth rate
assumption. Our price target is our base case value, derived from our residual income
valuation model. Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware companies within our
coverage, we think this methodology provides the most accurate value of the firm by

taking into account the cost of equity.
Our key assumptions are:
• Cost of equity of 7.8%:
° Risk-free rate of 1.0% (10-year Taiwanese government bond yield);
° Equity risk premium of 6.8%;
° Beta of 1.1;
• Medium-term growth rate of 16% (raised from 10%);
• Terminal growth rate of 3%.
Our price target implies 42.8x 2027e P/E and 31.7x 2028e P/E. These are above its five-
year historical range of 8x to 18x, which we view as justified considering its longer-term

transformation story to become a global total solutions provider, moving from passive
into active components. In addition, compared to its global passive component peers, we
believe Yageo stock remains undervalued with higher ROE and margins, but lower P/E vs.
Japanese peers (which trade on an average at 25x+ P/E).
Our bull and bear case scenario values increase by magnitudes similar to the change to
our base case value, to NT$1,530 and NT$505, respectively.

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C., Taipei
Branch
Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day: Ta Liang (3167.TW, NC); Increasing
PCB production difficulty , suggesting more industry testing/drilling
2 June 2026 | 9:59PM CST
We hosted meetings with Ta Liang (3167.TW, Not Covered), which is the key
high-end PCB production/testing equipment supplier in Taiwan, at our Taiwan
Computex & Corporate Day (June 1st). Key takeaways include:
(1) Increasing next-gen AI PCB production difficulty is driving

testing/measurement equipment demand: Management estimated the TM
(measurement) machine to PCB back-drilling machine ratio should be 1:8 in the
beginning of this year, but it starts seeing 1:6 design (vs. previous guidance of only
1:8) from customers, due mainly to the decreasing production yield for the next-gen
AI PCB, which, per our view, should imply a solid industry investment on this
testing/measurement equipment in coming quarters/years, considering the

increasing complexity of the PCB production process.
(2) Strong demand for 1.6T optical transceiver mSAP production equipment: The
company mentioned the demand outlook for 1.6T optical transceiver mSAP routing
solutions is strong, implying a solid PCB demand in coming quarters/years. Despite
that the final design of the 1.6T optical transceiver PCB routing equipment is not yet
finalized, and the company mentioned it has the dominant position in PCB routing

equipment with much higher than 50% market share, and is confident on its market
share in the 1.6T optical transceiver PCB equipment market.
(3) Capacity remains tight with full utilization, and outsourcing strategy and
de-bottleneck could further increase product shipment: Management mentioned
the current order is fully booked for the next 9 months while the effective monthly
capacity is ~200-300 units now (vs. previous guidance of 800 units of high end

machines). However, the company has no further plan to build new capacity, but it
will start outsourcing more low end product production to its outsourcing partner
from end of 2026 (plan to outsource ~50 units of low end equipment per month), to
work on more high end equipment in coming years. Moreover, the company
mentioned it is also pursuing efficiency improvements, with potential to achieve a
10% capacity uplift internally, although the company did not rule out the possibility

of capacity expansion through acquisitions, to have access to specialized labor.
(4) Semi-equipment business is scaling but still early phase: The company
mentioned it continues to make progress in semi inspection equipment, with 7 of its
solutions adopted in CoWoS and 2 in CoPoS with ASP ranging from low mn to the
teens of mn of NT$. The company is seeing OSAT demand rising (to account ~70% of
semi mix in 2026 vs. 50% in 2025), and the company is engaging with non-Taiwan

Read across to PCB suppliers: The company mentioned strong demand in 1.6T optical,
which is in line with the outlook from optical transceiver PCB suppliers (see here), while
we continue to expect a better adoption of the high-end mSAP technology, suggesting a
better outlook for the key mSAP suppliers. Management highlighted an improvement in
the TM to back-drilling machine ratio, which we believe is driven by higher PCB
measurement demand as the PCB yields remains low, and we believe the further

investment in PCB CAPEX could be expected from the industry in coming
quarters/years. We expect TM machine could become a potential bottleneck as demand
driven by low PCB yields may already outpace supply.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

## Document 104: 260602_hsbc_korea-ai-supply-chain

Source PDF: all report pdf/260602_hsbc_korea-ai-supply-chain.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-02
Pages: 17
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Issuer of report: The Hongkong and Shanghai 
Banking Corporation Limited, Seoul Securities 
Branch 
View HSBC Global Investment Research at: 
https://www.research.hsbc.com

◆ Tech companies are enjoying the AI boom; benefits from 
GPU/CPU processors, optical communication and 800V  
DC conversion  
◆ Memory earnings are the most solid, while equipment and 
materials earnings are about to take off 
◆ We prefer Samsung and EO Technics amid AI demand boom 
Key takeaways: We hosted our 2026 Korea tech tour on 27-29 May, with 14 companies 
across memory, semi equipment, materials, and the tech hardware supply chain. Most 
companies expressed an upbeat outlook: 1) memory/substrate/ MLCC are benefiting 
from CSP capex increases for agentic AI and tech upgrades to CPUs, optical 
interconnect and 800v power shift; 2) IT hardware is starting to encompass the robotic 
momentum; and 3) semi equipment and materials companies will benefit from aggressive 
capex and capacity expansion at memory makers. With robust demand and tight 
capacity, we are more constructive on the supply chain. Our key takeaways are below.  
Memory: CSP capex is soaring and demand catalysts are moving to CPU customers, 
as it is more feasible to manage AI agents vs GPU, and HBM3e prices will increase to 
narrow the gap with commodity. Robust x86 and ARM based CPU demand are boosting 
server and mobile DRAM prices; memory makers will enjoy output increases in 2H26 
and margin expansion as more LTA agreements will stabilize the earnings trend.  
Tech hardware – focus on FC-BGA: The package substrate sector is a clear AI 
beneficiary as well. High-end FC-BGA demand is running ahead of supply, signalling high 
utilisation and extending lead times; customers are seeking LTAs and co-investment to 
secure capacity. Structural drivers such as larger and higher layer bodies, silicon 
capacitor embedding, and glass core substrates are lifting content per unit, supporting a 
multi-year growth story.  
Semi equipment & materials – plenty of runway left: The tour brought fresh 
datapoints across our coverage. EO Technics is seeing laser full-cut beginning to 
displace mechanical dicing in HBM, alongside a sharp PCB drilling ramp and a 
debonder co-development with a leading foundry; HPSP is broadening its reach with a 
new DRAM customer expected in 3Q26, and NAND customer engagements. Park 
Systems is winning AFM traction in hybrid bonding and advanced packaging metrology; 
and Hansol Chemical is advancing precursor with optionality into the late-2026 patent 
expiry of hafnium based high-K precursors. Given the strong capex ramp, these point to 
content and customer expansion independent of bit-growth; we favour these moat and 
pricing power names where earnings momentum looks well supported.   
Our preference: We prefer Samsung Electronics in memory as we expect it to narrow 
the tech gap not only in HBM4 but also commodity DRAM vs peers. In semi equipment, 
we prefer EO Technics as it continues to see stronger demand of its laser equipment, 
through application expansion and new customer engagement at the front and back end.  
2 June 2026

Equities: Stock ratings and basis for financial analysis
HSBC and its affiliates, including the issuer of this report (“HSBC”) believes an investor's decision to buy or sell a stock should
depend on individual circumstances such as the investor's existing holdings, risk tolerance and other considerations and tha t
investors utilise various disciplines and investment horizons when making investment decisions. Ratings should not be used or
relied on in isolation as investment advice. Different securities firms use a variety of ratings terms as well as different r ating

systems to describe their recommendations and therefore investors should carefully read the definitions of the ratings used i n
each research report. Further, investors should carefully read the entire research report and not infer its contents from the  rating
because research reports contain more complete information concerning the analysts' views and the basis for the rating.
From 23rd March 2015 HSBC has assigned ratings on the following basis:

The target price is based on the analyst’s assessment of the stock’s actual current value, although we expect it to take six to 12
months for the market price to reflect this.  When the target price is more than 20% above the current share price, the stock  will
be classified as a Buy; when it is between 5% and 20% above the current share price, the stock may be classified as a Buy or a
Hold; when it is between 5% below and 5% above the current share price, the stock will be classified as a Hold; when it is between

5% and 20% below the current share price, the stock may be classified as a Hold or a Reduce; and when it is more than 20%
below the current share price, the stock will be classified as a Reduce.
Our ratings are re-calibrated against these bands at the time of any 'material change' (initiation or resumption of coverage, change
in target price or estimates).
Upside/Downside is the percentage difference between the target price and the share price.
s           ’s        s           s  pp            f         b s s:

For each stock we set a required rate of return calculated from the cost of equity for that stock’s domestic or, as appropria te,
regional market established by our strategy team. The target price for a stock represented the value the analyst expected the
stock to reach over our performance horizon. The performance horizon was 12 months. For a stock to be classified as Overweight,
the potential return, which equals the percentage difference between the current share price and the target price, including the

forecast dividend yield when indicated, had to exceed the required return by at least 5 percentage points over the succeeding 12
months (or 10 percentage points for a stock classified as Volatile*). For a stock to be classified as Underweight, the stock was
expected to underperform its required return by at least 5 percentage points over the succeeding 12 months (or 10 percentage
points for a stock classified as Volatile*).  Stocks between these bands were classified as Neutral.

*A stock was classified as volatile if its historical volatility had exceeded 40%, if the stock had been listed for less than 12 months
(unless it was in an industry or sector where volatility is low) or if the analyst expected significant volatility.  However, stocks which
we did not consider volatile may in fact also have behaved in such a way.  Historical volatility was defined as the past mont h's
average of the daily 365 -day moving average volatilities.  In order to avoid misleadingly frequent changes i n rating, however,

volatility had to move 2.5 percentage points past the 40% benchmark in either direction for a stock's status to change.

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
Agentic AI and CPU servers dominated the Computex keynote
discussions. Nvidia indicated that TSMC’s capacity can support
robust growth for Nvidia in 2027.
Agentic AI compute key focus: In their keynotes, Nvidia, Arm and Qualcomm

emphasized Arm-based server CPUs to support future agentic AI demand, echoing
our report, Global Tech: Agentic AI – The Surge Begins (11 May 2026).
Comments on Nvidia Vera CPU: Ahead of Computex, we published NVIDIA's Vera
CPU and Rubin GPU seen as the main show (27 May 2026). At today’s investor
luncheon, our Nvidia analyst Joe Moore asked CEO Jensen Huang the first question
about the US$20bn Vera CPU revenue assumption. Management indicated that

demand spans both AI server head nodes and stand-alone CPU servers. Given its
materially better performance than the x86 (see Exhibit 5 ), Nvidia can command
higher values for Vera, excl. memory sales. Our supply chain checks suggest 2.5mn–
4mn units are in preparation. Rubin has already entered fab production with no
apparent delays. On CPO (optics) vs. copper, Nvidia indicated that it will adopt CPO
only when necessary and rely on copper as long as possible.

Sufficient TSMC capacity:
At the 2024 and 2025 Computex events, we asked
Jensen Huang about TSMC’s pricing and value. This year, we asked whether Nvidia
can secure sufficient TSMC capacity for 2027 and whether allocation could affect
market share. We also asked – even more importantly – how Nvidia could secure
incremental allocation versus competitors.
Nvidia indicated it has sufficient TSMC capacity to support robust growth in 2027.
The company has convinced TSMC and other suppliers that demand remains strong.

Even as supply expands, demand will likely still exceed supply in 2027. Management
also emphasized that the AI semiconductor market is growing and does not
represent a zero-sum or pure market share competition.
Agenetic AI at the edge –  do we need a local LLM/agent:
Nvidia announced its RTX
Sparks AI PC WoA SoC in its keynote, jointly designed with MediaTek. We flagged
this 9 months ago in our MediaTek report, saying “Nvidia/MediaTek WoA AI PC keeps

being pushed out – now to 2H26. For N1X, we think the new timing of product launch
would be next year's Computex in June (vs. the CES in January), given that Microsoft's
new AI Windows system will be more of a 2H26 rollout.
Moreover, the new x86 SoC
will fuse an Intel CPU + Nvidia RTX GPU chiplet via NVLink". We do not see this
launch as a surprise. Investors are asking about the volume of RTX Sparks in 2026
and whether edge AI compute is necessary for Agentic AI.

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
June 2, 2026 04:22 PM GMT

Potential volume of Nvidia RTX Spark:
Our checks with PC brands at this year’s Computex suggest AI PCs with N1X will need to
price at US$2,899, while N1 models will be priced at US$1,799. We therefore estimate
~5mn–8mn units of N1X/N1 SoC shipments in 2026, contributing 5–10% of MediaTek’s
2026 EPS, assuming an average US$40 royalty fee per chip.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.
